# OpenSpec 逐 Requirement/Scenario crosswalk

- Query：逐项核对 `../openspec/specs` 的全部源 spec 与当前 Plateau/Servora Trellis 正文、源码和测试入口。
- Scope：mixed（历史 OpenSpec、当前正文、源码/测试入口）；旧 OpenSpec 仅作为对照来源。
- Date：2026-09-13

## 覆盖边界

- 源 spec：69；Requirement：467；Scenario：974。每个 Requirement 一行，Scenario 全部列出；无 Scenario 的 Requirement 仍保留空列表。
- 状态口径：主代理已实际运行 `go test ./security/... ./infra/... ./cmd/...`；该结果只作为背景，未将整组 Scenario 标为已实现并验证。其余 Servora、应用、Web、生成、Kafka/ClickHouse/Redis/OpenFGA/OIDC 联调均未在本轮运行。
- `已过时（范围外）` 仅用于 integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling；Audit scaffold 的目录、模块、Proto、Wire 分开判断。

## 逐项核对


### [business-repo-makefile-tooling](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md)

| 历史来源（Requirement/Scenario） | 当前目标主题 | 实际源码/测试证据入口 | 状态 | 判定/后续处理 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Shared Make Logic](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:9)<br>Scenario：[Make 仓库使用共享逻辑](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:13)<br>[Just 仓库不受 Make 文件要求约束](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:18) | [project/development.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/development.md)；[project/structure.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/structure.md) | 历史 Makefile/业务仓库约束；当前任务范围不启动迁移 | 已过时（范围外） | 该 Requirement“Shared Make Logic”的历史语义摘要：业务仓 Makefile 工具链 SHALL 仅适用于明确将 Make 声明为公共任务入口的仓库。适用时，仓库 SHALL 使用 make/core.mk 作为 root 与 service 可复用目标逻辑的共享来源。；它属于业务仓 Makefile/集成迁移，当前 Plateau 使用 Just，按任务范围不启动迁移，保留来源记录。 |
| [Project Generation Targets](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:23)<br>Scenario：[Make 仓库执行生成](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:27)<br>[Just 仓库迁移后执行生成](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:32) | [project/development.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/development.md)；[project/structure.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/structure.md) | 历史 Makefile/业务仓库约束；当前任务范围不启动迁移 | 已过时（范围外） | 该 Requirement“Project Generation Targets”的历史语义摘要：对于仍以 Make 作为公共任务入口的业务仓，Makefile 工具链 SHALL 保留项目级生成目标，并允许仓库扩展增加 API 语言和 Ent 流程；采用 Just 的仓库不需要通过 Make 暴露这些目标。；它属于业务仓 Makefile/集成迁移，当前 Plateau 使用 Just，按任务范围不启动迁移，保留来源记录。 |
| [API Target Semantics](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:37)<br>Scenario：[Root API generation](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:41)<br>[Service API generation](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:46)<br>[TypeScript output ownership](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:51) | [project/development.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/development.md)；[project/structure.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/structure.md) | 历史 Makefile/业务仓库约束；当前任务范围不启动迁移 | 已过时（范围外） | 该 Requirement“API Target Semantics”的历史语义摘要：业务仓 Makefile 工具链 SHALL define root and service API targets with explicit delegation semantics when API generation is enabled by make/extra.mk.；它属于业务仓 Makefile/集成迁移，当前 Plateau 使用 Just，按任务范围不启动迁移，保留来源记录。 |
| [Context-Specific Target Surface](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:56)<br>Scenario：[Repository root targets](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:60)<br>[Service directory targets](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:65)<br>[Root-only extensions](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:70) | [project/development.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/development.md)；[project/structure.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/structure.md) | 历史 Makefile/业务仓库约束；当前任务范围不启动迁移 | 已过时（范围外） | 该 Requirement“Context-Specific Target Surface”的历史语义摘要：业务仓 Makefile 工具链 SHALL expose different target surfaces for repository root and service-directory contexts.；它属于业务仓 Makefile/集成迁移，当前 Plateau 使用 Just，按任务范围不启动迁移，保留来源记录。 |

| [Removed Low-Value Targets](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:118)<br>Scenario：[Make 仓库移除低价值目标](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:122)<br>[Just 平台使用 leaf 开发入口](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:127) | [project/development.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/development.md)；[project/structure.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/structure.md) | 历史 Makefile/业务仓库约束；当前任务范围不启动迁移 | 已过时（范围外） | 该 Requirement“Removed Low-Value Targets”的历史语义摘要：对于仍以 Make 作为公共任务入口的仓库，工具链 SHALL 删除已明确废弃的低价值别名和下游特定目标；该要求不应被解释为要求 Just 仓库提供同名 Make target。；它属于业务仓 Makefile/集成迁移，当前 Plateau 使用 Just，按任务范围不启动迁移，保留来源记录。 |
| [Explicit Generated Artifact Cleanup](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:132)<br>Scenario：[Make 仓库执行 fresh generation](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:136)<br>[Just 平台执行生成清理](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/integration/business-repo/makefile-tooling/spec.md:141) | [project/development.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/development.md)；[project/structure.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/project/structure.md) | 历史 Makefile/业务仓库约束；当前任务范围不启动迁移 | 已过时（范围外） | 该 Requirement“Explicit Generated Artifact Cleanup”的历史语义摘要：对于仍以 Make 作为公共任务入口的业务仓，工具链 SHALL 使用显式 Make target 管理生成产物清理；采用 Just 的仓库可由其 Just capability 定义等价的清理接口。；它属于业务仓 Makefile/集成迁移，当前 Plateau 使用 Just，按任务范围不启动迁移，保留来源记录。 |

| 历史来源（Requirement/Scenario） | 当前目标主题 | 实际源码/测试证据入口 | 状态 | 判定/后续处理 |

| [仅 active 且邮箱已验证的 User 可登录](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:9)<br>Scenario：[Active User 使用正确密码登录](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:13)<br>[不可认证身份尝试登录](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:18) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“仅 active 且邮箱已验证的 User 可登录”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 使用 email 与密码认证 User。只有 active 且 email verified 的 User 可建立新的已认证登录状态；错误密码、未知 email、待验证和 disabled 状态 MUST 不建立新登录。公开失败结果 MUST 避免账号枚举。成功的会话身份认证 MUST 建立共享 Actor，其 Type 为 human，ID 为该 User 的稳定 IAM User ID。human 仅表示执行主体类别，不改变 User 的领域实体名称或存储模型。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [密码凭据使用抗离线破解存储](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:24)<br>Scenario：[创建或更新密码](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:27) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“密码凭据使用抗离线破解存储”的历史语义摘要：IAM MUST 使用 Argon2id 保存密码派生结果，并为每个密码使用独立随机 salt。明文密码 MUST NOT 被持久化、写入日志、放入 token claims 或通过管理 API 返回。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [Login Identifier 与 Authenticator 分离](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:32)<br>Scenario：[使用 email/password 登录](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:35) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“Login Identifier 与 Authenticator 分离”的历史语义摘要：IAM MUST 将“定位 User 的 Login Identifier”与“证明控制权的 Authenticator”作为不同生命周期。首版每个 User MUST 最多绑定一个全局唯一 email Login Identifier 和一个 active password Authenticator；email 匹配本身 MUST NOT 被视为认证成功。首版 MUST NOT 宣称支持 phone、WebAuthn 或 TOTP 登录。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [失败或未完成的 Authentication 不产生 Login Session](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:40)<br>Scenario：[Password Authentication 失败](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:44)<br>[Authentication Challenge 尚未完成](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:49) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“失败或未完成的 Authentication 不产生 Login Session”的历史语义摘要：Authentication attempt 与 Authentication Challenge 是短期认证过程，不是成功登录。HTTP 用户登录 MUST 只在所需凭据和当前用户状态检查全部成功后建立新的已认证绑定，并替换会话中的认证引用。已有会话的标准加载、保存或 idle 续期不等于凭据认证成功；失败请求不要求回滚这类普通会话活动，但 MUST NOT 创建或提升失败身份的权限。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [登录入口始终受基础限流保护](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:54)<br>Scenario：[正常登录请求](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:57) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“登录入口始终受基础限流保护”的历史语义摘要：email/password 登录 SHALL 经过服务端 rate limit。首版正常登录 MUST NOT 强制 CAP；注册、密码恢复和验证邮件重发的 CAP 要求由 account-management 定义。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [登录响应直接移除旧会话展示字段](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:62)<br>Scenario：[HTTP 登录成功响应](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:66) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“登录响应直接移除旧会话展示字段”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 保留 HTTP POST /v1/iam/authn/login。LoginResponse 正文只保留 user 字段，不返回旧 Session 展示资源或会话令牌；会话通过 HTTP Set-Cookie 交付。Proto SHALL 直接删除不再使用的字段和类型，不添加 reserved 或兼容字段，生成物与客户端 MUST 同步迁移。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [注销保留既有 HTTP 入口](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:71)<br>Scenario：[当前用户注销](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:75)<br>[注销操作失败](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:80) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“注销保留既有 HTTP 入口”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 保留 HTTP POST /v1/iam/sessions/current:logout。成功注销 MUST 完成当前登录及其关联用户 OAuth 状态撤销，并销毁会话、清除 Cookie。仅清空客户端状态不得被视为完成注销。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [内部服务调用使用独立服务身份](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:84)<br>Scenario：[有效服务身份进入受保护 RPC](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:88)<br>[受保护服务调用缺少有效证明](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:93) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“内部服务调用使用独立服务身份”的历史语义摘要：IAM 对内部服务调用 SHALL 验证服务访问 JWT，使用 gRPC Authorization metadata 的 Bearer 方式接收。认证成功后 SHALL 建立 Type=service, ID=<client_id> 的 Actor，其中 ID 来自已验证的机器访问令牌 sub，长期对应同一 OAuth 客户端；不要求或创建用户登录。原始身份 metadata、用户 Cookie 或仅能连接内网 MUST 不替代服务认证。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [服务认证拒绝不由接收端换取令牌](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:97)<br>Scenario：[过期访问令牌到达接收端](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:101) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“服务认证拒绝不由接收端换取令牌”的历史语义摘要：接收端 SHALL 拒绝已经过期的访问令牌，HTTP Bearer 入口返回 401，gRPC 返回 Unauthenticated。接收端 MUST 不持有调用方的客户端密钥，不代表调用方重新申请令牌，也不自动重放业务操作。身份有效但操作权限不足 SHALL 返回 403 或 PermissionDenied，不能将权限拒绝解释为需要续取。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [IAM 服务 JWT 认证明确类型与用途](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:106)<br>Scenario：[接收端不配置令牌类型](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:110)<br>[类型正确但用途或主体类别错误](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:115) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“IAM 服务 JWT 认证明确类型与用途”的历史语义摘要：IAM 服务认证器 SHALL 在构造时固定受信 issuer 与本服务 audience，并以代码规则检查 typ=JWT；本提案 MUST 不新增 expected_token_type 配置或对应 WithOption。IAM 应用声明适配 SHALL 在验证成功的声明上检查 token_use=access、actor_type=service 及非空 sub 与 client_id 相等，然后建立 service Actor。Actor 模型 MUST 不被视为需要可配置令牌头类型的原因。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [IAM 本地验签复用提供方公钥集合](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:119)<br>Scenario：[IAM 启动时 HTTP 端点尚未监听](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:123) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“IAM 本地验签复用提供方公钥集合”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 使用与自身 JWKS 发布器同源的本地公钥集合验证其签发的服务访问 JWT，并经过共享 JWT 认证与授权链。签名私钥 MUST 不复制到接收端 jwt 配置；本地公钥注入 MUST 不跳过 issuer、audience、typ、期限或主体校验。IAM 启动 MUST 不依赖启动前通过自身 HTTP JWKS 端点获取公钥。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [服务认证成功不跳过操作授权](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:128)<br>Scenario：[合法服务没有操作权限](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:132) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“服务认证成功不跳过操作授权”的历史语义摘要：已认证服务访问需要权限的 RPC 时，IAM MUST 检查该服务对目标资源与操作的许可。用户与服务可以使用相同的操作规则表达，但主体 MUST 来自已验证身份，不能由调用方在请求中指定。服务身份认证成功 MUST 不自动授予管理用户等业务权限。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [服务身份不替代当前用户](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:137)<br>Scenario：[服务调用当前用户操作](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:141) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“服务身份不替代当前用户”的历史语义摘要：要求当前用户语义的操作 MUST 使用经过验证的用户身份；内部服务 Actor MUST 不被转换为同名 User 或隐含管理员用户。HTTP 的用户自助入口继续可用；内部服务调用不维持用户 Cookie 会话。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [用户交互接口仅通过 HTTP 暴露](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:145)<br>Scenario：[服务调用用户管理 RPC](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:149)<br>[通过 gRPC 访问当前账户](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:154)<br>[通过 HTTP 访问用户管理接口](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:159)<br>[用户令牌访问管理 RPC](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/authn/spec.md:164) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“用户交互接口仅通过 HTTP 暴露”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 通过 HTTP 保留 AuthnService、SessionService 和 AccountService。UserService 的创建、查询、列表、更新、禁用和启用六个管理 RPC SHALL 仅通过 gRPC 暴露并使用服务 JWT 认证，其 HTTP 绑定、注册及对应生成接口 SHALL 删除；用户登录、当前会话及当前账户接口 MUST 不注册到 gRPC。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [Provider 发布一致的 discovery 与 JWKS](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:9)<br>Scenario：[RP 发现 Provider](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:12) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“Provider 发布一致的 discovery 与 JWKS”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 在 /.well-known/openid-configuration 发布与实际 issuer、authorization endpoint、token endpoint、userinfo endpoint 和 JWKS endpoint 一致的 metadata。OIDC 标准端点 MUST NOT 添加 Plateau /v1 前缀。JWKS SHALL 发布用于验证 RS256 签名的公开 key，并通过稳定 kid 支持 key 选择。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [Confidential OAuth client 使用 Authorization Code 与 PKCE S256](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:17)<br>Scenario：[Confidential OAuth client 兑换授权码](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:20)<br>[Authorization code 被复用](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:25) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“Confidential OAuth client 使用 Authorization Code 与 PKCE S256”的历史语义摘要：用户授权流程中的 confidential OAuth client MUST 使用 Authorization Code flow 和 PKCE S256。authorization request MUST 校验已注册 client_id、精确匹配的 redirect URI、response type、scope、state、code challenge 与 challenge method；token exchange MUST 校验一次性 authorization code、对应 code verifi；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [第一方静态 client 自动通过授权确认](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:30)<br>Scenario：[trusted client 请求不允许的 scope](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:33) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“第一方静态 client 自动通过授权确认”的历史语义摘要：受控 seed 的第一方 OAuthClient MAY 被标记为 trusted。对 trusted client，IAM 在用户登录成功后 MUST 自动批准 client 配置允许且请求声明的 scopes，不显示授权确认页面；未注册、未允许或不支持的 scope MUST 仍按 OAuth 错误语义拒绝。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [Provider scope 集合固定且 client 逐项限制](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:38)<br>Scenario：[client 请求允许的标准 scope](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:41)<br>[第一方 BFF 请求允许的 scopes](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:46) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“Provider scope 集合固定且 client 逐项限制”的历史语义摘要：用户授权流程中，Provider MUST 固定支持 openid、profile、email、offline_access 四个标准 scope，不从运行时配置扩展全局 scope 集合。每个静态 OAuthClient MUST 通过 allowed_scopes 声明允许请求的 scope；authorization request 的 scope MUST 同时属于 Provider 固定集合和该 client 的允许集合。offline_access 表示 client 请求在 User 不在线时使用 r；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [未认证或不可用 User 不得完成授权](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:51)<br>Scenario：[未登录浏览器访问 authorize](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:55)<br>[用户或登录已经失效](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:60) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“未认证或不可用 User 不得完成授权”的历史语义摘要：authorization endpoint SHALL 要求从共享 HTTP 会话解析出的有效 IAM 登录，并检查当前用户状态及适用的撤销事实；不得依赖会话中持久化的完整 User。待邮箱验证、disabled 或登录已撤销的 User MUST NOT 完成授权或获得 code。普通 HTTP 会话续期不等于该身份重新有效。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [UserInfo 只返回已授权标准 claims](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:78)<br>Scenario：[使用 openid profile email scope 查询 UserInfo](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:81) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“UserInfo 只返回已授权标准 claims”的历史语义摘要：userinfo endpoint MUST 要求有效 access token，并只返回其 scope 允许的 OIDC 标准 claims。User disabled、token 过期、issuer 或 audience 不匹配时 MUST 拒绝请求。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [OIDC profile claims 只来自已定义 User 字段](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:86)<br>Scenario：[profile scope 返回已存字段](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:89) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“OIDC profile claims 只来自已定义 User 字段”的历史语义摘要：首版 OIDC claims SHALL 只从 IAM User 的已定义字段生成：稳定 sub、email、email_verified，以及 UserProfile 中的 name、given_name、family_name、nickname、preferred_username、picture、locale。未设置的可选字段 MUST NOT 伪造默认值；IAM MUST NOT 返回 address、phone、Tenant、Membership、角色、权限或任意 metadata claim。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [Refresh token 每次使用都轮换](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:94)<br>Scenario：[正常 refresh 轮换](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:97)<br>[复用已轮换 refresh token](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:102) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“Refresh token 每次使用都轮换”的历史语义摘要：每次成功 refresh MUST 使旧 refresh token 失效并签发同一 OAuthClient Token Session 的新 refresh token。检测到已使用 refresh token 再次出现时，IAM MUST 拒绝请求并撤销对应 Token Session，防止重放继续扩散。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [OAuth client 仅通过静态 seed 注册](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:107)<br>Scenario：[同一应用获准使用用户与机器流程](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:111)<br>[客户端没有声明允许的授权类型](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:116)<br>[未注册 client 发起授权](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:120)<br>[服务客户端不配置用户登录回调](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:125) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“OAuth client 仅通过静态 seed 注册”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 从受控配置或 seed 加载客户端，并声明稳定客户端 ID、客户端认证材料和非空 allowed_grant_types。该配置 SHALL 复用现有数据模型的授权类型并映射 OIDC 库，不新增 kind 来区分所谓用户客户端与服务客户端。OAuth 客户端 SHALL 始终表示应用，允许的授权类型决定它可发起哪些流程，实际完成的流程决定本次令牌的用户或服务主体。用户授权码客户端 SHALL 配置精确 redirect URI、response type 与允许的用户 scope；服务客户端；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [首版只支持明确列出的核心 flow](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:130)<br>Scenario：[Client 请求 implicit flow](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:134)<br>[服务发现机器授权能力](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:139) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“首版只支持明确列出的核心 flow”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 保留用户 Authorization Code、PKCE S256、refresh、userinfo、discovery 与 JWKS，并增加客户端凭据授权用于服务自身访问。implicit、hybrid、device authorization、token exchange、JWT profile 和 dynamic client registration MUST 不被宣称支持；Discovery SHALL 与实际启用的授权流程一致。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [OIDC 只在浏览器交互中使用 HTTP 会话](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:143)<br>Scenario：[浏览器继续授权](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:147)<br>[token endpoint 收到 Cookie](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:152) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“OIDC 只在浏览器交互中使用 HTTP 会话”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 为现有 authorize、认证继续 callback 和浏览器 logout 交互接入同一 HTTP 会话管理器和身份解析能力。token、userinfo、introspection、revoke、discovery 与 keys MUST 不因请求携带 Cookie 而加载或续期浏览器会话。本阶段不新增应用级 Origin/Referer 或 CSRF 门禁，既有 OAuth/OIDC 协议验证和 CORS 行为继续保留。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [OIDC 认证时间与会话续期分离](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:157)<br>Scenario：[会话续期后签发 token](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:161)<br>[refresh 后签发 ID token](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:165) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“OIDC 认证时间与会话续期分离”的历史语义摘要：OIDC auth_time SHALL 来自实际成功认证事件，不得取 HTTP 会话的最近访问、Cookie 更新时间或 token 刷新时间。普通 HTTP 会话活动与 refresh 轮换 MUST 不被解释为重新认证。用于 OAuth 撤销关联的业务登录引用 MUST 不暴露会话令牌，也不因令牌更新失去关联。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [保留 OAuth 事务与撤销业务](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:169)<br>Scenario：[当前登录注销](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:173)<br>[已使用刷新令牌被重放](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:178)<br>[注销与授权码兑换交错](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:183)<br>[撤销先于换码提交](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:187)<br>[换码先于撤销提交](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:192)<br>[撤销与刷新交错](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:197) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“保留 OAuth 事务与撤销业务”的历史语义摘要：授权请求、state、nonce、PKCE、授权码单次消费和 refresh family SHALL 继续由既有协议实现管理，不迁入通用 HTTP 会话载荷。当前注销、密码安全事件和用户禁用对关联 OAuth 状态的撤销 MUST 保持有效，不以通用会话的销毁、续期或枚举结果替代业务判断。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [服务通过 OAuth 申请自身访问令牌](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:202)<br>Scenario：[服务申请访问令牌](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:206)<br>[客户端没有机器授权资格](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:211) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“服务通过 OAuth 申请自身访问令牌”的历史语义摘要：已注册且允许机器授权的客户端 SHALL 能使用客户端 ID 与密钥向现有 token endpoint 申请短期 JWT 访问令牌。该流程 MUST 不依赖浏览器登录、授权码或用户会话，不签发用户 ID Token 或用户刷新令牌。申请失败 MUST 不产生可用令牌。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [OAuth 客户端 ID 长期标识服务主体](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:215)<br>Scenario：[服务更新密钥后继续调用](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:219)<br>[服务主体与同名用户并存](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:224) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“OAuth 客户端 ID 长期标识服务主体”的历史语义摘要：同一 IAM 签发方下，允许机器授权的 OAuth 客户端 SHALL 以稳定 client_id 长期标识服务主体；机器访问令牌的 sub SHALL 等于该 client_id。IAM MUST 不要求独立 Service 身份表或 service_id 绑定。客户端 ID 由受控配置管理，不能由令牌申请方任意指定主体值。用户授权流程的 sub SHALL 继续标识 User，不能因请求具有 client_id 就解释为服务身份。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [服务令牌使用五分钟有效期与本地验证](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:229)<br>Scenario：[服务被禁用时已有令牌尚未过期](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:233)<br>[令牌有效但权限被撤销](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:238) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“服务令牌使用五分钟有效期与本地验证”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 通过 oidc.service_access_token_ttl 配置机器访问令牌的有效期，未配置时为五分钟，显式值 MUST 为正时长。签发 SHALL 据此设置 exp，不能被客户端请求覆盖；本提案按五分钟配置验收。接收端 SHALL 在本地验证身份，不为每次请求调用在线令牌查询或服务状态检查。禁用服务 SHALL 阻止后续新签发；已签发令牌的身份效力允许保留到原有效期届满。每次业务操作的授权 SHALL 独立执行，令牌仍有效不等于操作必须放行。到期后客户端 SHALL 使用自身客户端认证重；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [调用端复用标准令牌缓存与到期续取](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:242)<br>Scenario：[缓存令牌进入更新余量](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:248)<br>[令牌申请失败](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:253)<br>[同时发起多个正常续取调用](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:258)<br>[业务端返回认证拒绝](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:262) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“调用端复用标准令牌缓存与到期续取”的历史语义摘要：本提案的独立 HTTP/gRPC 测试客户端 SHALL 在初始化时创建并复用 Go OAuth2 的机器授权 TokenSource，并在发送业务请求前通过标准传输适配取得访问令牌。无缓存、临近到期或已经到期时 SHALL 由 SDK 使用客户端自身认证重新申请，成功后才携带新令牌发送本次业务请求。续取 MUST 不依赖用户刷新令牌，不自行实现定时刷新循环，也不放入接收端 security/jwt 公钥缓存。 令牌申请 SHALL 使用有界 HTTP 超时和应用生命周期控制。申请失败 SHALL 返回调用错误，不；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [机器令牌明确服务访问用途与目标](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:266)<br>Scenario：[用户 ID Token 被用于服务调用](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:272)<br>[服务按注册目标申请令牌](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:276)<br>[请求参数试图扩大注册目标](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:281)<br>[服务客户端没有有效目标](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:285) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“机器令牌明确服务访问用途与目标”的历史语义摘要：IAM 机器访问 JWT SHALL 使用 RS256 与 typ=JWT，并提供可验证的 iss、sub、aud、iat、exp、jti 和 client_id，以及 token_use=access、actor_type=service。用途和主体类型 SHALL 来自 IAM 机器授权语义，不接受申请方任意指定；sub SHALL 等于 client_id。接收端 MUST 同时验证服务用途、主体类别及主体一致性，不能因 JWT 类型或签名有效而把用户 ID Token 当作服务令牌。本提案不宣称符合 RFC；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [IAM 发布供服务本地验签的 JWKS](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:290)<br>Scenario：[接收服务首次验证 IAM 服务令牌](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:294)<br>[提供方发现结果无效](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:299)<br>[接收服务更新公钥集合](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/oidc-provider/spec.md:303) | [iam-service/oidc.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/oidc.md)；[iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“IAM 发布供服务本地验签的 JWKS”的历史语义摘要：IAM Discovery SHALL 继续公布可信的 jwks_uri，JWKS SHALL 发布允许用于验签的公钥及其实际 kid，并与服务访问 JWT 的签名密钥匹配。响应 MUST 不包含签名私钥。接收服务 SHALL 能按受信 issuer 经现成 Discovery 函数取得 jwks_uri，再通过本提案的共享 JWT 组件获取、缓存和刷新该集合，在本地完成验证，不把 JWKS 获取等同于逐请求在线令牌查询。常规接收端配置 SHALL 只要求 issuer 和自身 audience；公钥来源未显式指定；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [IAM 管理身份与认证事实](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:9)<br>Scenario：[业务服务需要认证用户](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:13)<br>[服务更新客户端密钥](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:18) | [iam-service/authorization.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/authorization.md)；[plateau/security/authZ.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/security/authZ.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“IAM 管理身份与认证事实”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 负责其拥有的用户、服务身份、外部身份关联、认证材料和会话策略。服务身份 SHALL 长期使用受控 OAuth client_id，通过已验证的服务访问令牌映射为 service Actor；用户流程 SHALL 使用稳定 IAM User ID 映射为 human Actor。共享 security 包 SHALL 提供基础身份合同和认证授权接线，其业务生命周期与签发策略 SHALL 由 IAM 拥有。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [本地 ActorContext 由目标服务可信构建](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:23)<br>Scenario：[请求伪造租户角色](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:27) | [iam-service/authorization.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/authorization.md)；[plateau/security/authZ.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/security/authZ.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“本地 ActorContext 由目标服务可信构建”的历史语义摘要：目标业务服务 MAY 根据共享 Actor 从自身数据库、缓存或受控业务规则构建本地 ActorContext。调用方传入的 tenant、membership、role、permission 或 scope 字段 MUST 被视为不可信提示，除非目标服务自行验证并加载。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [跨服务身份使用 wire contract，不绑定认证实现 claims 类型](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:32)<br>Scenario：[Resource Server 本地验证 JWT](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:36)<br>[Resource Server 定义本地 claims decoder](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:41)<br>[opaque token 使用 introspection](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:46)<br>[同一 Actor 进入不同业务服务](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:51) | [iam-service/authorization.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/authorization.md)；[plateau/security/authZ.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/security/authZ.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“跨服务身份使用 wire contract，不绑定认证实现 claims 类型”的历史语义摘要：跨服务调用 SHALL 传播能够表达稳定 Actor Type 与 ID 的认证语义。每个 token issuer MAY 在 JWT Registered Claims 基础上定义自己的 claims 扩展；Resource Server MUST 为自己消费的 token 定义本地最小 claims decoder，但 MUST NOT 要求所有 issuer 或服务导入 security/authn/jwt 中的共享业务 claims struct。本 change 共享的是 issuer 发布的 JWT/；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [业务服务拥有自身资源授权](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:56)<br>Scenario：[业务服务检查资源关系](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:60)<br>[服务令牌允许发送给 IAM](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:65) | [iam-service/authorization.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/authorization.md)；[plateau/security/authZ.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/security/authZ.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“业务服务拥有自身资源授权”的历史语义摘要：业务服务 SHALL 负责自身 Tenant、Membership、Role、Customer、业务资源和关系的生命周期及授权解释。IAM SHALL 管理调用服务对 iam:global 的用户管理权限；该权限 MUST 不自动授予 Admin 或其他业务资源的访问资格，业务域的用户权限也 MUST 不替代 IAM 服务权限。OAuth 客户端注册、受众配置和成功认证 MUST 不代替目标服务的操作授权。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [用户入口与服务调用分别验证实际执行主体](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:69)<br>Scenario：[用户管理员身份未随服务调用传播](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/security-boundary/spec.md:73) | [iam-service/authorization.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/authorization.md)；[plateau/security/authZ.md](../../../../../spec/plateau/security/authZ.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“用户入口与服务调用分别验证实际执行主体”的历史语义摘要：IAM HTTP 用户入口 SHALL 使用用户会话身份；内部 gRPC SHALL 使用服务身份并检查服务权限。服务主体 MUST 不被隐式转换为用户，也不代表调用链中某个用户已经获得授权。未来业务服务在用户入口执行用户授权后调用 IAM，IAM SHALL 继续独立验证调用服务及其操作资格。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

### [plateau/iam/session Specification](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md)

| 历史来源（Requirement/Scenario） | 当前目标主题 | 实际源码/测试证据入口 | 状态 | 判定/后续处理 |

| [只有成功 Authentication 才创建 Login Session](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:9)<br>Scenario：[Authentication 成功后建立 Session](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:13)<br>[Authentication 尚未完成](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:18) | [iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md)；[iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“只有成功 Authentication 才创建 Login Session”的历史语义摘要：IAM MUST 只在 User 完成所需 Authentication 后建立新的已认证登录状态。每次登录 SHALL 绑定单一 User，并保留必要的稳定 OAuth 关联、真实认证时间与撤销事实；不透明会话令牌不是公开登录标识。HTTP Cookie 携带本应用的会话令牌，内部服务调用不消费浏览器会话；会话可以在认证前存在，只有通用状态存在或发生续期 MUST NOT 被视为登录成功。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [IAM Login Session 与 OAuth Token Session 分离](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:23)<br>Scenario：[一个登录状态授权多个 BFF](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:27)<br>[Token refresh 不延长浏览器登录](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:32) | [iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md)；[iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“IAM Login Session 与 OAuth Token Session 分离”的历史语义摘要：同一 User 的新登录 MUST NOT 自动撤销其他独立登录。OAuth authorization code 兑换产生的 Token Session SHALL 按 OAuthClient 维护独立 refresh family，并关联建立授权时的登录事实；一个登录可以关联多个 OAuthClient。经 HTTP 更新会话令牌不等于 OAuth 授权更新，Token Session 的轮换不得隐式改变其他 client 的授权或续期该会话。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [Login Session 使用 idle 滑动期限与 absolute 上限](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:37)<br>Scenario：[有效 Session 在 idle 窗口内再次访问 IAM](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:43)<br>[Session 超过 idle 期限后再次访问](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:48)<br>[身份依赖暂时故障](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:53) | [iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md)；[iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“Login Session 使用 idle 滑动期限与 absolute 上限”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 通过 HTTP 会话管理能力执行 idle 和 absolute 期限，默认分别为 7 天与 30 天。身份认证仍 MUST 检查当前用户与登录撤销状态，不为旧展示资源维护重复的期限字段。 已接入的 HTTP 请求加载有效会话后，可按相同会话语义处理 idle 活动，包含只读访问、业务错误或身份校验失败的请求。常规续期 MUST 不重置 absolute 上限；只有成功登录或明确重新认证才能通过更新令牌开启新的认证生命周期。会话续期不得恢复已撤销身份或把失败认证解释为成功。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [浏览器 Session Cookie 具有安全属性](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:58)<br>Scenario：[生产环境建立浏览器 Session](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:61) | [iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md)；[iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“浏览器 Session Cookie 具有安全属性”的历史语义摘要：IAM Login Session MUST 使用名为 __Host-iam_session 的 opaque Cookie，并设置 HttpOnly、Secure、Path=/、host-only 与 SameSite=Lax。Cookie MUST NOT 包含明文密码、User 信息、access token、refresh token 或 Session metadata。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [注销只撤销当前 Login Session 及其 Token Sessions](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:66)<br>Scenario：[多会话 User 注销当前浏览器](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:70)<br>[旧请求在注销后保存数据](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:75) | [iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md)；[iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“注销只撤销当前 Login Session 及其 Token Sessions”的历史语义摘要：HTTP 注销 SHALL 在同一数据库事务中撤销当前登录及其关联 OAuth Token Session，提交后再销毁当前会话，向对应客户端交付 Cookie 清除指示；其他独立登录保持有效。成功结果 MUST 表示这些必需操作已经完成，重复请求安全失败或保持已撤销状态。会话数据的物理存在、晚到保存或会话令牌更新 MUST 不恢复已撤销身份。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [业务状态与必要撤销原子提交](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:80)<br>Scenario：[密码更新过程中的撤销失败](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:86)<br>[注销撤销提交后会话销毁失败](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:91) | [iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md)；[iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“业务状态与必要撤销原子提交”的历史语义摘要：密码变更、密码重置和用户禁用 SHALL 各自在同一数据库事务中完成业务状态更新及其必需的登录和用户 OAuth 状态撤销；密码重置事务 SHALL 同时消费一次性重置令牌。biz SHALL 定义业务规则及原子 Repo 操作，data SHALL 实现这些接口并拥有具体事务；biz MUST 不导入 data，Repo 接口 MUST 不暴露 Ent 或 SQL 事务类型。 SCS SHALL 在请求开始时装载会话；与业务变更关联的会话写入、令牌更新或销毁 SHALL 在业务事务提交后执行。SCS 存储操作独立；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [会话机制与 IAM 业务事实不重复管理](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:96)<br>Scenario：[用户被禁用后继续访问](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:100)<br>[密码操作的撤销范围](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:105) | [iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md)；[iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“会话机制与 IAM 业务事实不重复管理”的历史语义摘要：通用会话 SHALL 只持久化必要的身份引用，不保存完整 User 或请求上下文。IAM SHALL 保留当前身份状态、真实认证时间及必要的撤销和 OAuth 关联事实，但不重复管理通用令牌、Cookie 或 idle 期限，也不要求其他微服务采用相同登录模型。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [格式切换不兼容旧会话](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:110)<br>Scenario：[升级后收到旧 Cookie](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/session/spec.md:114) | [iam-service/sessions.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/sessions.md)；[iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“格式切换不兼容旧会话”的历史语义摘要：开发期的新会话格式允许直接重建受影响存储，使旧会话及关联的用户 OAuth 状态失效，不通过兼容读取恢复旧身份，不要求维护版本化数据库迁移或历史会话搬迁。初始化或重建 MUST 限定受影响表，保留无关用户和密码数据；HTTP 调用方同步使用新响应字段。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [IAM User 是全局唯一身份](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/user-management/spec.md:9)<br>Scenario：[重复 email 创建 User](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/plateau/iam/user-management/spec.md:12) | [iam-service/identity.md](../../../../../spec/iam-service/identity.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [IAM account service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/account.go)；[IAM authn service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/authn.go)；[IAM user service](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/service/user.go)；[`Validate`/`ServiceActor`](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/jwt.go)；[session authenticator](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/authn/session.go)；[OIDC provider](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/provider.go)；[service token](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/oidc/service_token.go)；[account integration test](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/account_integration_test.go)；[HTTP/session integration tests](../../../../../../app/iam/service/internal/server/http_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“IAM User 是全局唯一身份”的历史语义摘要：IAM SHALL 维护全局 User，不得按业务 Tenant、Membership 或 Realm 复制身份。每个 User SHALL 拥有稳定且不可复用的 UUIDv7 唯一标识；同一 email MUST NOT 同时绑定多个有效 User。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
