| [contrib SHALL own optional ecosystem runtime packages](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:9)<br>Scenario：[External client wiring enters contrib](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:13)<br>[Mail remains proto-only](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:18)<br>[Existing ecosystem packages remain under contrib](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:23) | [servora/framework/providers.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/providers.md)；[servora/framework/architecture.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/architecture.md) | [contrib Kafka client](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/kafka/kafka.go)；[contrib Redis client](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/redis/redis.go)；[contrib ownership](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/AGENTS.md) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“contrib SHALL own optional ecosystem runtime packages”的历史语义摘要：servora/contrib SHALL own Servora 提供的可选第三方生态接线，而不是新增 servora/infra。支持布局 SHALL 继续包含现有 database、cache、Kafka 与 Kubernetes packages；Mail SHALL 继续只提供 Proto 配置，不创建同名 runtime 包。产品平台自行拥有的安全引擎与 provider 接线 MUST NOT 进入 Servora contrib。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [contrib runtime packages SHALL separate base clients from higher-level adapters](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:27)<br>Scenario：[CRUD adapter is isolated from base Ent package](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:35)<br>[Redis cache strategy is distinct from Redis client wiring](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:40)<br>[Kafka has no audit ownership](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:45)<br>[Redis provider uses Proto configuration without logger coupling](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:49)<br>[Kafka native logging is explicitly opted in](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:54)<br>[Provider errors retain their underlying cause](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:60) | [servora/framework/providers.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/providers.md)；[servora/framework/architecture.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/architecture.md) | [contrib Kafka client](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/kafka/kafka.go)；[contrib Redis client](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/redis/redis.go)；[contrib ownership](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/AGENTS.md) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“contrib runtime packages SHALL separate base clients from higher-level adapters”的历史语义摘要：Base contrib packages SHALL 提供官方外部 Client 构造与 provider-native 类型，不承载下游业务语义。更高层 Adapter SHALL 按其实现的 Servora capability 组织，而不是随意嵌入 base client package。CRUD Adapter、cache strategy 与 Kafka wiring MUST 继续保持既有分层；Servora contrib MUST 不接纳 Plateau 自有安全 Adapter。 Base cl；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [contrib governance SHALL be documented in AGENTS](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:66)<br>Scenario：[New contributor reads contrib guidance](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:70) | [servora/framework/providers.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/providers.md)；[servora/framework/architecture.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/architecture.md) | [contrib Kafka client](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/kafka/kafka.go)；[contrib Redis client](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/redis/redis.go)；[contrib ownership](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/AGENTS.md) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“contrib governance SHALL be documented in AGENTS”的历史语义摘要：servora/contrib/AGENTS.md SHALL 将 contrib 定义为 Servora 可选生态集成空间，说明 base client 与 capability Adapter 的分层，以及禁止业务语义、Servora Audit schemas、具体 Audit backend、retention、storage DDL 和新 servora/infra/** packages。该文档 MUST 不再把 Plateau 自有安全 provider 列为 Servora contrib 成员。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [缓存 JSON 迁移保持 key 与历史值稳定](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:90)<br>Scenario：[等价 map 输入生成相同 key](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:94)<br>[新 reader 读取历史 JSON cache value](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:100)<br>[不兼容编码不会隐式改变 key](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/contrib/runtime-packages/spec.md:106) | [servora/framework/providers.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/providers.md)；[servora/framework/architecture.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/architecture.md) | [contrib Kafka client](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/kafka/kafka.go)；[contrib Redis client](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/redis/redis.go)；[contrib ownership](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/AGENTS.md) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“缓存 JSON 迁移保持 key 与历史值稳定”的历史语义摘要：Servora cache strategy 在 JSON 编解码实现迁移期间 SHALL 保持相同 logical cache input 的 key 计算确定性，不得因 map 遍历顺序或等价 JSON 表示产生随机 key。迁移后的 JSON cache reader SHALL 能读取迁移前写入且仍在有效期内的兼容缓存值；无法保持历史 key 或值合同的调用点 MUST 明确保留兼容编码路径。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [IDENTIFIER、OUTPUT_ONLY 与 INPUT_ONLY 由 service Plan 执行](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/api-contract/spec.md:154)<br>Scenario：[OUTPUT_ONLY 输入被忽略](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/api-contract/spec.md:158)<br>[INPUT_ONLY 响应清除](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/api-contract/spec.md:163) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`ResourcePlan`](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/plan.go)；[`ResourceNameMatcher`/`Parse`/`Format`](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/name.go)；[CRUD error constructors](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/errors.go)；[plan tests](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/plan_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“IDENTIFIER、OUTPUT_ONLY 与 INPUT_ONLY 由 service Plan 执行”的历史语义摘要：Create body 中的 IDENTIFIER name SHALL 清除并忽略；Update 中 name 只用于解析目标 identity。OUTPUT_ONLY 值在 Create/Update 输入中 SHALL 递归清除，mask 直接、父路径或 * 命中都忽略且不报错。INPUT_ONLY SHALL 正常参与输入 validation 与 mutable 写入，但所有返回 resource 的响应 SHALL 由 service Plan 递归清除。Biz/data MUST NOT 读取 fie；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [update_mask 省略、显式与 `*` 的确定语义](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/api-contract/spec.md:196)<br>Scenario：[隐式 mask 的父 message 整体替换](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/api-contract/spec.md:200)<br>[显式 mask 表达 Clear](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/api-contract/spec.md:205)<br>[`*` 与其它路径混用失败](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/api-contract/spec.md:210)<br>[`*` 下 REQUIRED 缺失失败](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/api-contract/spec.md:215) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`ResourcePlan`](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/plan.go)；[`ResourceNameMatcher`/`Parse`/`Format`](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/name.go)；[CRUD error constructors](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/errors.go)；[plan tests](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/plan_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“update_mask 省略、显式与 `*` 的确定语义”的历史语义摘要：省略 mask 时 SHALL 按 AIP-134 从已填充顶层字段生成隐式 mask：无 presence scalar 仅非默认值进入；optional scalar 与 oneof member 只要 present 就进入；message present 进入父路径（整体替换全部可变后代，不递归缩成叶子）；repeated/map 仅非空进入。显式 mask 命中时：无 presence scalar 默认值为 Set(default)；optional/message absent 为 Clear；rep；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [Ent wildcard 方言 lowerer 保持 binary 语义与参数化](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:68)<br>Scenario：[SQL pattern 元字符保持 literal](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:76)<br>[SQLite 大小写敏感](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:81)<br>[JSONPath 与普通列语义一致](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:86)<br>[match-all 不匹配 NULL](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:91) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`ListFields`/`Bind`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_fields.go)；[query binding](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/query_binding.go)；[`Clear`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/clear.go)；[Ent `List`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution.go)；[Ent list tests](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“Ent wildcard 方言 lowerer 保持 binary 语义与参数化”的历史语义摘要：Ent adapter SHALL 为 PostgreSQL、MySQL、SQLite 生成语义等价的 typed wildcard predicate，并保持既有 UTF-8 binary、区分大小写与重音、无 Unicode normalization 的 string profile。Exact SHALL 继续使用现有 binary =/!=，MUST NOT 退化为 LIKE。 PostgreSQL 与 MySQL 的 Prefix、Suffix、Contains SHALL 使用参数化 LIKE/NOT；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [ListFields 一次性构造并返回 error](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:96)<br>Scenario：[配置错误启动失败](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:100)<br>[构造成功后不可变](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:105) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`ListFields`/`Bind`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_fields.go)；[query binding](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/query_binding.go)；[`Clear`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/clear.go)；[Ent `List`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution.go)；[Ent list tests](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“ListFields 一次性构造并返回 error”的历史语义摘要：Ent NewListFields SHALL 以一次性 constructor options 组合全部 field binding、默认顺序、cursor key 与 custom extension，形如 NewListFields&#91;PO&#93;(Columns(validColumn), Bind(...), DefaultOrder(...), CursorKey(...))，完成静态校验后返回 (*ListFields, error)，成功后对象不可变。系统 MUST NOT 采用 constructor；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [cursor 值原生提取](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:119)<br>Scenario：[普通列免写 extractor](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:123)<br>[custom order 缺 extractor 构造失败](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:128) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`ListFields`/`Bind`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_fields.go)；[query binding](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/query_binding.go)；[`Clear`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/clear.go)；[Ent `List`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution.go)；[Ent list tests](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“cursor 值原生提取”的历史语义摘要：绑定普通模型列后，Ent adapter SHALL 使用隐藏 SELECT alias 与 generated entity Value(name) 等 Ent 原生机制取得 final-order cursor 值；调用方 MUST NOT 被要求编写只返回同一字段的 getter。Relation、computed、JSON expression、数据库函数、自定义 sort-key expression 等 custom order binding MUST 显式提供 typed value extract；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [adapter 执行边界](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:133)<br>Scenario：[业务事务内组合 CRUD 组件](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:137)<br>[CRUD 包不暴露事务 API](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:142) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`ListFields`/`Bind`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_fields.go)；[query binding](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/query_binding.go)；[`Clear`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/clear.go)；[Ent `List`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution.go)；[Ent list tests](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“adapter 执行边界”的历史语义摘要：Ent CRUD adapter SHALL 只执行类型化 predicate、order、cursor、skip 与可选 Count，并以 page_size + 1 探测下一页；数据库连接、方言与 SQL 执行 SHALL 由 Ent client/driver 负责。Adapter 接收什么 Ent builder/client 就在其连接或事务上执行，MUST NOT 感知、开启或提交事务；core/crud MUST NOT 持有 client 或从 context 获取事务。本变更的 CRUD 生态 M；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [后端错误原样透传](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:147)<br>Scenario：[唯一约束冲突原样返回](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:151)<br>[token 失配仍报框架错误](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:156) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`ListFields`/`Bind`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_fields.go)；[query binding](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/query_binding.go)；[`Clear`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/clear.go)；[Ent `List`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution.go)；[Ent list tests](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“后端错误原样透传”的历史语义摘要：core/crud 与 Ent CRUD adapter MUST NOT 识别、归类或包装后端数据库错误：执行失败时 SHALL 原样返回 Ent/driver 错误链（errors.Is/As 可达），MUST NOT 内建方言错误码表或提供归类挂载点。存储事实（not-found、唯一/约束冲突、不可用）的判断与对外表达 SHALL 由业务分层完成：data 用 Ent 原生 helper 或 biz 哨兵识别，biz 以业务错误 proto 表达语义。例外边界：adapter/框架代码对自身合同照常报框架错；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [软删除模型级合同](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:175)<br>Scenario：[默认查询排除已删除记录](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:179)<br>[Delete 改写为标记](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:184)<br>[显式 bypass 访问已删除记录](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:189)<br>[Mixin 不自动开启 AIP-164](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/backend-adapters/spec.md:194) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`ListFields`/`Bind`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_fields.go)；[query binding](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/query_binding.go)；[`Clear`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/clear.go)；[Ent `List`](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution.go)；[Ent list tests](../../../../../../../servora/contrib/db/entgo/crud/list_execution_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“软删除模型级合同”的历史语义摘要：存储层软删除 SHALL 为模型级一次性启用：Ent schema 采用 Servora SoftDeleteMixin，MUST NOT 作为 driver 全局开关，NewRepo 不重复配置。字段集合 SHALL 为 nullable delete_time、非空 string deleted_by、nullable purge_time（后两者仅预留存储，框架 MUST NOT 自动填充或计算）；默认只为 delete_time 建单列索引。Mixin SHALL 使用 Ent 官方模式的 query in；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [唯一 cursor key 显式声明](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:165)<br>Scenario：[缺失 cursor key 配置启动失败](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:169)<br>[客户端顺序自动补全 cursor key](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:174) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`PrepareList`/`ListQuery`](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/list.go)；[filter parser](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/filter.go)；[order parser](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/order.go)；[page token codec](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/page_token.go)；[list tests](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/list_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“唯一 cursor key 显式声明”的历史语义摘要：Repository MUST 为标准 List 显式配置一个或多个后端字段作为 cursor key；adapter MUST NOT 从 ORM 主键元数据自动推断。每个 key term SHALL 显式声明默认方向；构造时 SHALL 验证可稳定排序、可从查询结果提取值、存储非空；配置不成立时应用启动失败。默认顺序与客户端显式顺序 SHALL 最终包含完整 cursor key：已映射到同一后端字段的不重复追加，缺失项按配置顺序与方向追加。Page token SHALL 保存全部 final order；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [内建排序类型语义](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:188)<br>Scenario：[enum 按 number 排序](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:192) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`PrepareList`/`ListQuery`](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/list.go)；[filter parser](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/filter.go)；[order parser](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/order.go)；[page token codec](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/page_token.go)；[list tests](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/list_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“内建排序类型语义”的历史语义摘要：.Order() SHALL 为显式开放的单值叶子提供内建跨后端语义：string 按 UTF-8 binary；bool 按 false < true；enum 按 Proto enum number；integer/float 按数值；Timestamp 按时间点；Duration 按时长。Bytes、普通 message、map 与 repeated 字段 MUST NOT 自动支持排序。后端存储表示不能直接复现资源类型语义时，adapter MUST 使用确定性等价表达；无法同时实现 order 与 key；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [特殊字符串排序经业务 sort-key](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:197)<br>Scenario：[profile 变更使旧 token 失效](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:201) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`PrepareList`/`ListQuery`](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/list.go)；[filter parser](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/filter.go)；[order parser](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/order.go)；[page token codec](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/page_token.go)；[list tests](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/list_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“特殊字符串排序经业务 sort-key”的历史语义摘要：需要忽略大小写、重音折叠、拼音或 locale 字典序时，业务模型 MUST 维护稳定、可索引的后端 sort-key，ListFields 将资源字段的 order 能力显式绑定到该 sort-key 并声明稳定、带版本的 profile ID；adapter 对 key 继续使用内建 binary order 与 keyset，MUST NOT 执行语言学转换。Profile ID SHALL 进入 page-token fingerprint；排序算法变化时 MUST 更换 profile ID。Sort-k；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [page_token 与 skip 组合语义](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:206)<br>Scenario：[token 加 skip 组合翻页](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:210)<br>[逐页改变 page_size 不失效](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:215) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`PrepareList`/`ListQuery`](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/list.go)；[filter parser](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/filter.go)；[order parser](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/order.go)；[page token codec](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/page_token.go)；[list tests](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/list_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“page_token 与 skip 组合语义”的历史语义摘要：标准 List SHALL 允许同时提供 page_token 与 skip：无 token 时 skip 从集合稳定顺序起点跳过；有 token 时先恢复类型化 cursor 再额外跳过 skip 条。skip SHALL 表示资源数量而非页数。Query fingerprint SHALL 绑定资源、collection、filter、final order 与业务 scope，MUST NOT 绑定 page_size 或当前请求的 skip。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [CursorValue 强类型校验](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:314)<br>Scenario：[SQLite 文本时间跨页保留原始 offset](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:320)<br>[arm 与 logical type 失配被拒绝](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:326)<br>[篡改的越界值被拒绝](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/crud/list-query/spec.md:331) | [servora/framework/crud.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/crud.md)；[service/backend/crud.md](../../../../../spec/service/backend/crud.md) | [`PrepareList`/`ListQuery`](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/list.go)；[filter parser](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/filter.go)；[order parser](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/order.go)；[page token codec](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/page_token.go)；[list tests](../../../../../../../servora/core/crud/list_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“CursorValue 强类型校验”的历史语义摘要：CursorValue SHALL 使用无字段名 oneof（string、int64、uint64、double、bool、bytes、Timestamp、Duration、NullValue），只按 final order 位置解释：signed int32/int64 与 enum number 用 int64 arm，unsigned int32/uint64 用 uint64 arm，float32/float64 用 double arm 并按目标 logical type 校验范围。Decode 后访；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [contrib 聚合根的准入闸](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/package/organization/spec.md:9)<br>Scenario：[新 backend client 加入 contrib](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/package/organization/spec.md:15)<br>[含业务语义的包被拒绝](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/package/organization/spec.md:20)<br>[Kafka remains transport-only](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/package/organization/spec.md:24) | [servora/framework/architecture.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/architecture.md)；[servora/framework/providers.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/providers.md) | [Servora package ownership](../../../../../../../servora/AGENTS.md)；[`ProviderSet`](../../../../../../../servora/core/bootstrap/provider.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“contrib 聚合根的准入闸”的历史语义摘要：contrib/ runtime members SHALL 只包含 Servora 选择提供的可选生态集成或第三方系统接线。它们 MAY 提供官方 Client 构造、配置映射、生命周期、日志、tracing、健康检查和明确属于 Servora capability 的 Adapter，但 MUST NOT 嵌入下游业务语义、产品平台安全策略或 Servora Audit event schema。 外部系统操作本身 MUST 不赋予 contrib 对业务 Audit、retention、table DDL、t；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [Public proto comments MUST describe durable schema semantics](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/proto/comment-hygiene/spec.md:9)<br>Scenario：[配置 proto 不复述 section key](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/proto/comment-hygiene/spec.md:13)<br>[公共 proto 不记录旧项目兼容叙事](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/proto/comment-hygiene/spec.md:18)<br>[framework proto 不写下游仓库职责](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/proto/comment-hygiene/spec.md:24)<br>[注解 proto 保留插件消费说明](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/proto/comment-hygiene/spec.md:30)<br>[字段级非显然语义保留](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/proto/comment-hygiene/spec.md:36) | [servora/proto/annotations.md](../../../../../spec/servora/proto/annotations.md)；[servora/proto/generation.md](../../../../../spec/servora/proto/generation.md)；[api/proto/generation.md](../../../../../spec/api/proto/generation.md) | [Proto ownership](../../../../../../../servora/api/protos/AGENTS.md)；[Buf module/lint config](../../../../../../../servora/buf.yaml)；[Buf generation config](../../../../../../../servora/buf.go.gen.yaml) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“Public proto comments MUST describe durable schema semantics”的历史语义摘要：公共 proto 注释 SHALL 描述长期有效的 schema、字段、协议或插件消费语义。注释 MUST NOT 承载历史迁移说明、旧项目兼容叙事、下游仓库职责划分、显而易见的“这是配置”横幅，或对 servora.conf.v1.section key 的重复说明。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [文档来源及选择确定一致](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/transport/api-docs/spec.md:30)<br>Scenario：[多文档切换](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/transport/api-docs/spec.md:34)<br>[远程文档由浏览器读取](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/transport/api-docs/spec.md:39)<br>[非法来源被提前拒绝](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/transport/api-docs/spec.md:44)<br>[显式列表完全替代单文档路径](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/transport/api-docs/spec.md:48) | [servora/framework/transport.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/transport.md)；[servora/framework/tls.md](../../../../../spec/servora/framework/tls.md) | [API docs handler](../../../../../../../servora/transport/server/http/apidocs/apidocs.go)；[API docs tests](../../../../../../../servora/transport/server/http/apidocs/apidocs_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“文档来源及选择确定一致”的历史语义摘要：系统 MUST 支持文件路径、程序内字节或绝对 HTTP/HTTPS URL 三种来源，每份显式文档 MUST 恰有一种非空来源。documents 非空时 MUST 完整替代顶层单文档 path，不读取被替代的路径；列表为空时 MUST 使用 path 的配置值或默认值。多文档 MUST 有唯一、稳定的标识符，按输入顺序展示选择项，默认选中第一份。文件和程序内字节的多文档 MUST 通过 {basePath}/{slug}/openapi.yaml 分别访问；单份远程文档 MUST 不伪造本地原始文档端点。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

| [ApiError 保存 transport 已确认的错误事实](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:32)<br>Scenario：[HTTP adapter 构造结构化错误](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:43)<br>[应用区分 network 与 timeout](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:49)<br>[应用表达主动取消](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:55)<br>[保留底层原因](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:61) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [`ApiError`/error mapper](../../../../../../../servora/web/packages/proto-utils/src/errors.ts)；[proto-utils error tests](../../../../../../../servora/web/packages/proto-utils/test/errors.test.mjs) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“ApiError 保存 transport 已确认的错误事实”的历史语义摘要：ApiError SHALL 继承标准 Error，其 name SHALL 为 ApiError，并 SHALL 保存以下只读事实： - kind MUST 是 http、network、timeout 或 cancelled 之一。 - service 与 method SHALL 保存构造 adapter 已确认的逻辑操作元数据；生成 ClientTransport 使用生成调用元数据，手写 HTTP adapter 使用自身稳定的操作标识。 - httpStatus 与 responseBody SHALL；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [未知 Kratos error body 接受严格运行时校验](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:66)<br>Scenario：[解析最小合法 Kratos body](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:77)<br>[解析带 metadata 和额外字段的 body](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:82)<br>[拒绝无效核心字段](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:88)<br>[拒绝无效 metadata](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:94)<br>[安全处理任意未知输入](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:99) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [`ApiError`/error mapper](../../../../../../../servora/web/packages/proto-utils/src/errors.ts)；[proto-utils error tests](../../../../../../../servora/web/packages/proto-utils/test/errors.test.mjs) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“未知 Kratos error body 接受严格运行时校验”的历史语义摘要：parseKratosErrorBody(value: unknown) SHALL 无副作用地检查未知值，并且仅在以下条件全部满足时返回 KratosErrorBody： - 输入是非 null、非数组对象。 - code 是有限整数；parser MUST NOT 把数字字符串转换为 number。 - reason 与 message 是 string。 - metadata 缺省，或者是非 null、非数组对象且每个可枚举自有属性值都是 string。 parser SHALL 允许 envelope 携带；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [ApiError mapper 仅解析 HTTP response body](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:105)<br>Scenario：[从 HTTP ApiError 提取 Kratos body](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:111)<br>[非 HTTP 错误不解析 body](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:116)<br>[reason 精确匹配](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:122) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [`ApiError`/error mapper](../../../../../../../servora/web/packages/proto-utils/src/errors.ts)；[proto-utils error tests](../../../../../../../servora/web/packages/proto-utils/test/errors.test.mjs) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“ApiError mapper 仅解析 HTTP response body”的历史语义摘要：parseKratosError(error: ApiError) SHALL 仅对 kind: "http" 的错误调用 parseKratosErrorBody(error.responseBody)。network、timeout、cancelled、缺少 response body 或结构无效的错误 SHALL 返回 null。 isKratosReason(error: ApiError, reason: string) SHALL 基于 parseKratosError 的结果执行区分大小写的完整字符串；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [错误识别和展示策略由应用拥有](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:128)<br>Scenario：[参考应用展示已知业务错误](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:136)<br>[合法但未映射的 enum reason](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:143)<br>[未知与非 HTTP 错误](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/proto-utils-errors/spec.md:149) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [`ApiError`/error mapper](../../../../../../../servora/web/packages/proto-utils/src/errors.ts)；[proto-utils error tests](../../../../../../../servora/web/packages/proto-utils/test/errors.test.mjs) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“错误识别和展示策略由应用拥有”的历史语义摘要：proto-utils SHALL NOT 识别 FetchError、AxiosError、DOMException 或其它第三方错误，也 MUST NOT 把 Kratos reason 直接转换为用户文案。应用 SHALL 在拥有 HTTP client 与产品上下文的位置识别外部错误、显式构造 ApiError，并决定 reason 到 i18n 或本地文案的映射。 plateau/app/example/web SHALL 从 @servora/proto-utils/errors 消费 ApiError；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |

### [TypeScript 错误 reason 生成](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md)

| [错误生成器以兼容默认值选择 Go 或 TypeScript target](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:9)<br>Scenario：[现有 Go 模板不传 target](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:15)<br>[显式选择 Go target](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:21)<br>[选择 TypeScript target](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:26)<br>[拒绝未知 target](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:32) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [error generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/main.go)；[TypeScript error generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/generate_ts.go)；[TypeScript integration tests](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/typescript_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“错误生成器以兼容默认值选择 Go 或 TypeScript target”的历史语义摘要：protoc-gen-go-errors SHALL 接受 plugin 参数 target=go\\|ts。未设置 target 时 SHALL 默认为 go；缺省调用与显式 target=go MUST 产生字节级一致的现有 *_errors.pb.go 输出、状态码校验、无注解跳过行为与错误文本。target=ts SHALL 只生成本规格定义的 TypeScript companion，不生成 Go 文件。任何其它 target SHALL 在生成期返回明确错误，MUST NOT 产生部分输出。 binary；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [TypeScript target 只选择声明 Servora error option 的顶层 enum](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:38)<br>Scenario：[enum default 覆盖全部 values](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:44)<br>[只有单个 value 声明 code](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:50)<br>[普通 enum 不生成 companion](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:56)<br>[两个 target 共享非法 code 校验](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:61) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [error generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/main.go)；[TypeScript error generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/generate_ts.go)；[TypeScript integration tests](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/typescript_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“TypeScript target 只选择声明 Servora error option 的顶层 enum”的历史语义摘要：顶层 enum 在声明 servora.errors.v1.default_code，或者至少一个 value 声明 servora.errors.v1.code 时，SHALL 被识别为 error enum。普通未注解 enum 与 nested enum MUST NOT 触发 TypeScript error companion。 Go 与 TypeScript target SHALL 共享同一 error option 解析和 HTTP code 校验，所有有效 code MUST 位于 100..59；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [TypeScript sidecar 与 package index 共享确定性输出目录](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:66)<br>Scenario：[标准 package 目录生成 sidecar](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:72)<br>[一个文件包含多个错误枚举](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:79)<br>[两个 plugin 使用不同输出根](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:85) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [error generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/main.go)；[TypeScript error generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/generate_ts.go)；[TypeScript integration tests](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/typescript_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“TypeScript sidecar 与 package index 共享确定性输出目录”的历史语义摘要：对于至少包含一个已识别 error enum 的源文件，target=ts SHALL 使用 file.GeneratedFilenamePrefix + ".errors.ts" 生成一个 source-relative sidecar。一个源文件内的多个 error enums SHALL 汇总到同一 sidecar，顺序保持 Proto 声明顺序。 protoc-gen-go-errors target=ts 与 protoc-gen-typescript-http SHALL 位于同一 TypeScript；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 历史 Node16/NodeNext bundler 条件与当前 proto-utils 的 bundler 配置不一致；兼容目标不能写成当前配置事实。 |

| [framework reason sidecar 通过语义化 npm 子路径发布](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:133)<br>Scenario：[导入 CRUD framework reason](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:139)<br>[内部文件布局保持封装](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-error-reason-codegen/spec.md:145) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [error generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/main.go)；[TypeScript error generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/generate_ts.go)；[TypeScript integration tests](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-go-errors/typescript_integration_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“framework reason sidecar 通过语义化 npm 子路径发布”的历史语义摘要：@servora/proto-utils SHALL 通过 ./error-reasons/* 条件 export 公开编译后的 reason sidecar，其中 types 映射到 ./dist/gen/*.errors.d.ts，import 映射到 ./dist/gen/*.errors.js。通配符 SHALL 支持多级 Proto source path，公共 specifier MUST NOT 暴露 dist、src 或内部重复后缀布局。 包 SHALL 继续是 pure ESM 且声明 sideE；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 历史 Node16/NodeNext bundler 条件与当前 proto-utils 的 bundler 配置不一致；兼容目标不能写成当前配置事实。 |

| 历史来源（Requirement/Scenario） | 当前目标主题 | 实际源码/测试证据入口 | 状态 | 判定/后续处理 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [TypeScript 类型与 HTTP client 的唯一生成器](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:9)<br>Scenario：[CRUD helper 复用而不重复 transport](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:15)<br>[普通方式生成错误枚举类型](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:21)<br>[error sidecar 复用唯一联合类型](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:27)<br>[按 package 输出 index.ts](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:34) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [TypeScript HTTP generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/main.go)；[HTTP plugin generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/internal/plugin/generate.go)；[HTTP generator tests](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/internal/plugin/generate_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“TypeScript 类型与 HTTP client 的唯一生成器”的历史语义摘要：protoc-gen-typescript-http SHALL 是 Proto message/enum/request/response TypeScript 类型、transport 与 HTTP client 的唯一生成器，并 SHALL 继续按 Proto package 输出独立 index.ts。它 SHALL 把 FileToGenerate 中的错误 reason enum 当作普通顶层 enum 生成字符串联合类型，MUST NOT 读取或解释 servora.errors.v1.default；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [ProtoJSON 64 位整数无损表示](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:40)<br>Scenario：[total_size 为 optional string](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:44)<br>[大整数往返无损](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:49) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [TypeScript HTTP generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/main.go)；[HTTP plugin generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/internal/plugin/generate.go)；[HTTP generator tests](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/internal/plugin/generate_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“ProtoJSON 64 位整数无损表示”的历史语义摘要：生成的 TypeScript 类型 SHALL 按 ProtoJSON 合同把 64 位整数（int64/uint64/sint64/fixed64/sfixed64）表示为十进制 string，MUST NOT 使用 JavaScript number 承载，避免超出安全整数范围时精度丢失。标准 List 的 optional int64 total_size SHALL 对应 optional 十进制 string。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [HTTP 路径变量展开与编码合同](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:54)<br>Scenario：[资源名作为路径变量展开](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:58)<br>[name helper 不做 URL 编码](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:63) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [TypeScript HTTP generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/main.go)；[HTTP plugin generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/internal/plugin/generate.go)；[HTTP generator tests](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/internal/plugin/generate_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“HTTP 路径变量展开与编码合同”的历史语义摘要：生成的 HTTP client SHALL 按 google.api.http 注解展开路径变量：单段变量 {var} 与多段变量 {var=**} 按注解模板放入 URL path。路径变量值 SHALL 由 client 执行百分号编码，编码规则 SHALL 保留多段资源名变量中的 / 分隔符语义；资源名 helper（Go/TS XxxName）MUST NOT 承担 URL 编码职责。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
| [生成物纳入正式生成链](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:68)<br>Scenario：[page token 类型进入默认生成物](../../../../../../../openspec/specs/servora/web/typescript-http-codegen/spec.md:72) | [servora/web/proto-utils.md](../../../../../spec/servora/web/proto-utils.md)；[web/client/http.md](../../../../../spec/web/client/http.md) | [TypeScript HTTP generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/main.go)；[HTTP plugin generator](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/internal/plugin/generate.go)；[HTTP generator tests](../../../../../../../servora/cmd/protoc-gen-typescript-http/internal/plugin/generate_test.go) | 代码或配置存在但未验收 | 该 Requirement“生成物纳入正式生成链”的历史语义摘要：protoc-gen-typescript-http SHALL 纳入仓库统一 TypeScript 生成链（buf.typescript.gen.yaml 与 make gen.ts），生成物随 @servora/proto-utils 生态发布流程管理；servora.crud.v1 公共 Proto（含 PageTokenPayload）SHALL 进入默认 TypeScript 生成物，MUST NOT 设置专用 exclude_paths 例外。；证据栏给出当前对应源码符号、配置或测试入口。当前仅确认入口存在，未执行本 Req 全部 Scenario，故保留未验收；后续按 Scenario 定向验证并只迁移仍有效的规则。 |
